# critique-stops-on-convergence

- State: parked
- Priority: 1
- Sequence: 87
- Risk: severity=2 novelty=2 exposure=2 accumulation=2 basis="Governs every critique loop's spend and its stop (severity 2); a new decision rule in dispatch (novelty 2); every seat and adopter's critique lane (exposure 2); runs on every loop (accumulation 2)"
- Tier: 2
- Intent: Critique loops stop on a trusted per-tier criterion, few rounds being the norm (Wido 2026-10-02). Tier 1: no critique required; one is allowed when asked for (today tier 1 refuses every critic role), with a HARD cap of 1 round and none of the guidance machinery; a material finding that cannot be fixed locally means the tier was wrong and the goal moves to tier 2. Tiers 2 and 3: before a loop starts, its brief names the threat model and the rabbit-hole risks with mitigations (short at tier 2, required at tier 3); dispatch decides each round from recorded material counts (zero = stop, else continue); the steward watches every loop and alerts the AGENT running the critique, never the human (past the tier's watch threshold e.g. round 3 at tier 2, count not falling, findings mostly in the last fold), each alert carrying advice drawn from the loop's own named risks (match findings to a risk, give its mitigation as the next step) so the loop converges under guidance; if that fails the loop runs into the far-away cap (20) and only there does failure bubble up to the human, with the signals and advice given. The steward records each loop's end cause (criterion, guided convergence, cap, re-tier) so trust in the criterion is measured.
- Origin: main
- Next step: TIER 3, PROPER DESIGN FIRST (Wido 2026-10-03). (1) Design page with Part 1 intent, threat model and the rabbit-hole risks of the stop criterion itself with mitigations; gather the evidence: tonight's loops (m1f one-folder reads 1, 4, 3 material; m1h landing-deploys-the-engine 8 then 4; fleet-panel-ux 5, 5, 1 then 4,3,2,2,2), the per-round material counts the engine records, and every rule in skills/design-critique/SKILL.md, skills/code-critique/SKILL.md, docs/orchestration.md and internal/protocol/templates/review-brief.md that decides when a loop stops or asks the person. (2) Astra critique tier 3. (3) Build in slices: the skill and doc rules first (failsafe round, divergence-to-human, unconfirmed fold), then the engine and steward parts. All ADDED notes below are requirements. INPUT (2026-10-03, close-path matrix 54a620329): a chain whose earlier findings were accepted, fixed and not raised again by a clean later round now closes, but its register is not 'clean' by readsubject.CleanRegister, so a CODE chain closed that way yields no landable read. Decide in this design whether a fix confirmed by a clean later round counts as resolved for landing. Also: the 'omitted findings stay open' rule (plans/defect-analysis-gate-design.md:127) was kept; the matrix resolves accepted-and-not-re-raised findings from each round's own decisions file instead. INPUT 2026-10-03 (m1f one-folder, stop rule fired after the class audit existed): the recurring findings were on the audit's list but assigned to later works; the reader counted one against the current work because that work touched the file. Rule to design: once a class is mechanical, the check's assigned list is the reader's scope authority (a site assigned to a named later work is out of scope for this work), and the goal's landing acceptance is the check at zero; a deferral inside the goal is safe only with the committed ratchet. INPUT 2026-10-03 18:17 (m1e, from m1f's one-folder stack): the engine treats every unfixed material finding, refuted and out-of-scope included, as needing a person's accept-risk, so a goal built as twelve stacked works produces a batch of person acts per work. Design the rule: a finding dispositioned out-of-scope to a NAMED later work of the same goal, while a ratchet or landing condition holds the goal from landing with it open, closes without a person; a refutation with evidence that names where the behaviour is checked closes the same way; a person is asked only when no later owner or no hold exists. Applied today as a standing policy for one-folder in Wido's word. MEASURED 2026-10-03 (Wido: optimise without changing behaviour): critic rounds are 14% of the day's tokens; the costly pattern is a preliminary read per fix round before the formal review (m1h: nine on one unit), against the rule of 2026-09-17 'one read per build'; design the loop so a build gets one independent read and a fix round is read once more only when the finding was material. RULE INPUT 2026-10-03 19:20 (Wido: 'are these still building the smallest thing that works?'): the design critic's first finding class is scope: does step 1 name the smallest first use, with everything else on a deferred list? A finding about surface the deferred list holds is out of scope for step 1 and does not count toward convergence; m1h's deploy design (4,176 words, 15 failure rows, pause/rollback/resume concurrency) drew nine preliminary reads on corners its first real use never reaches before the seat subtracted them. WIDO RULED 2026-10-03 19:25 ('yes to these too. Again: through machinery because these are vital to get right'): a design page's step 1 section is at most 1,500 words and everything else is a deferred list; the design critic's first finding class is scope (does step 1 name the smallest first use?) and findings on deferred surface do not count toward convergence. Design the rule here (its audit is the same shape as the AGENTS.md word budget), critique it at this goal's tier, and build it so the critic and the page audit enforce it; the critic's tool-call cap stays as it is (not lowered). Today's drafts: 4,176 words (landing-deploys-the-engine), 4,621 (agent-works-as-project-partner).
